# VENPOD Completion Ledger

> Deprecated for the terrain holes/pop-in work as of 2026-05-14. This broad
> ledger remains historical evidence for prior sparse-renderer, dome, water,
> and validation work, but the active terrain fix is now tracked in
> `docs/TERRAIN_STREAMING_COMPLETION_LEDGER.md`. Do not use this file as the
> primary closure gate for the current "holes / slow pop-in / half-loaded LOD"
> issue.

Status vocabulary:

- `DONE_VERIFIED`: implementation exists and current evidence proves the claimed behavior.
- `DONE_UNVERIFIED`: implementation appears to exist, but current evidence is incomplete or stale.
- `PARTIAL`: some implementation and/or tests exist, but the original requirement is not fully satisfied.
- `NOT_STARTED`: no meaningful implementation was found.
- `BLOCKED`: cannot proceed without missing information, assets, credentials, hardware, or a user decision.
- `DEFERRED_BY_USER_ONLY`: only the user may assign this status.

This ledger is stricter than `docs/reference/sparse-completion-audit.md`. A green
smoke gate is evidence only for the behavior it actually checks. In particular,
the current capture gates prove continuity/no unsafe near misses; they do not
prove that the whole visible world is rendered as coherent voxel terrain.

## Render Diagnosis From Supplied Video

Generated artifacts:

- `VENPOD/build/diagnostics/render_issue_2026-05-11_160928/contact_sheet_1fps.png`
- `VENPOD/build/diagnostics/render_issue_2026-05-11_160928/contact_sheet_2fps.png`
- `VENPOD/build/diagnostics/render_issue_2026-05-11_160928/frame_0001.png` through `frame_0031.png`

Observed:

- The old hard circular gap/moat is mostly fixed.
- Close foreground terrain can render as real sparse voxel surfaces.
- The majority of the view is still often mid-height/far-height/far-SVO continuity terrain, not coherent near/mid voxel geometry.
- Below or near water level, the view becomes dominated by a flat blue/water/sky-like fill. This is consistent with `RaymarchFarWater` and far/background fallback behavior, not a proper submerged voxel scene.
- The terrain reads as layered heightmap/fog fallback with spikes and smear-like far features, especially during movement and wide views.
- The current ownership gates can report zero `miss` and zero `unsafeNearMiss` while this visual failure remains, so they are insufficient as final completion gates.

Probable technical causes to investigate next:

- `RaymarchBackgroundField` falls through from sparse surfaces to mid voxel, mid height, far SVO, far height, then `RaymarchFarWater`; ownership success does not mean voxel-surface coherence.
- `RaymarchFarWater` is a far-plane/background hit, not volumetric water or submerged voxel traversal.
- `SampleGeneratedSurfaceVoxel` intentionally strips mid/far interior volume, so mid/far visual layers are shell/height continuity rather than whole-world voxel authority.
- Sparse surface culling/distance limits leave much of the view to the background pass.
- The completion gates validate ownership categories and nonblank frames, but not material/LOD coherence, underwater correctness, or the percentage of visible terrain represented by real voxel surfaces.

## Native-Frame Diagnosis From 2026-05-14 Recording

Generated artifacts:

- `..\.codex-video-frames\screen-20260514-155020-analysis\contact_1fps.jpg`
- `..\.codex-video-frames\screen-20260514-155020-analysis\contact_5fps.jpg`
- `..\.codex-video-frames\screen-20260514-155020-analysis\seq_000_089.jpg` through `seq_810_899.jpg`
- `..\.codex-video-frames\screen-20260514-155020-analysis\high_change_neighborhoods.jpg`
- `..\.codex-video-frames\screen-20260514-155020-analysis\metrics.json`
- `..\.codex-video-frames\screen-20260514-155020-analysis\second_summary.json`

Video facts:

- The source is `1918 x 1078`, H.264, fixed `30 fps`, `900` video frames, `30.0` seconds.
- The native-frame sequence shows the largest engine-side discontinuities around frame `57` (`1.90s`), frame `387` (`12.90s`), frames `597-682` (`19.90s-22.73s`), and frames `738-832` (`24.60s-27.73s`).
- Frames from about `842` (`28.07s`) onward contain Windows start-menu overlays and must be excluded from engine visual acceptance.

Observed current issues:

- Startup/warmup exposes sparse residency: the first `~1.9s` show terrain as thin horizon strips and isolated fragments, followed by a one-frame pop into a fuller mid/far scene.
- The renderer still exposes holes between sparse surface, mid voxel, mid height, far SVO, far height, and water/background layers. The view often reads as suspended shells, detached islands, and replacement fill rather than one coherent terrain volume.
- A large pale-blue circular dome appears during steep camera pitch from about `19.9s` onward. This matches the project's own brush-preview failure comment in `VENPOD/assets/shaders/Graphics/PS_Raymarch.hlsl`: stale brush state near the camera can project as a giant fisheye dome and hide the sparse surface pass. `VENPOD/assets/shaders/Graphics/PS_Overlay.hlsl` contains a similar overlay path, so both paths must be audited.
- The near ownership sphere / background start logic around `SurfaceAuthoritativeBackgroundStartForRay` can become visually legible instead of staying an invisible handoff rule.
- Far terrain lacks coherent occlusion and silhouettes: distant cliffs become flat curtains, incomplete backsides, or water/sky-filled voids.
- Current technical gates can remain green while these artifacts remain visible. The missing validation is temporal, same-frame layer provenance, and public-quality visual acceptance.

Obsidian concept notes that apply directly:

- `Voxel LOD Seam Contract`: the current failures are consistency failures at resolution and layer boundaries; the engine needs explicit seam rules for surface crossing, material priority, and no holes/double surfaces.
- `Screen Error Driven Voxel Streaming`, `Brick Residency`, and `Voxel IO and Upload Backpressure`: fixed-distance or budget-only residency is insufficient; visible contribution, camera motion, edit focus, and consequence must shape request priority.
- `Voxel Runtime Failure Semantics`: missing, stale, low-resolution, and derived visual data need distinct fallback semantics. A background fill must not silently masquerade as authoritative terrain.
- `Surface Extraction Boundary` and `Sparse Volume Query Planning`: sparse surfaces, mid voxel, SVO, and water/background layers need named query contracts. The renderer should choose the cheapest valid representation, not whichever layer happens to return a hit.
- `Screen-Space Detail Bound`: a pixel-covered surface shell is not proof of world completeness. Visual LOD can be screen-error-driven, but collision/edit/ownership authority cannot be inferred from it.
- `Voxel Validation Fields` and `Validation Cameras and Aesthetic Gates`: final acceptance needs validation cameras and localized failure causes, not only JSON counters or ownership totals.

Engine-level attack plan:

1. Make every visible pixel explainable: record or infer the winning layer, fallback reason, freshness, and whether it is authoritative, derived, or placeholder.
2. Treat brush preview and ownership volumes as overlays with hard public-frame invariants: no preview or ownership boundary may cover more than a small screen fraction unless the user is actively editing and the target is valid.
3. Replace "fallback fills holes" with a seam contract: near sparse surface, mid voxel, far SVO, far height, and water must have explicit entry/exit, overlap, material, and occlusion rules.
4. Drive residency by screen error plus consequence: visible near holes, crosshair rays, future camera motion, waterline views, and steep-pitch visibility get priority over speculative distant fill.
5. Add native-frame temporal gates: detect one-frame layer snaps, large circular overlays, and sudden whole-frame ownership swaps at 30 fps before visual review.
6. Add same-camera normal/debug review pairs for the exact problematic routes in the May 14 recording.
7. Only after those gates exist should implementation changes be made and validated.

### R-021
1. Requirement ID: R-021
2. Requirement text: Sparse near terrain is visibly coherent.
3. Source document / source location: `refactor.md` Definition Of Done; `docs/reference/sparse-refactor-review.md`.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `PS_SparseSurface.hlsl`, `VS_SparseSurface.hlsl`, `SparseSurfaceExtractor`, `SparseSurfaceGpuResources`
6. What currently exists: Close sparse surfaces render as voxel geometry; surface smokes verify fragments. Mid-voxel clipmap rendering is now default, remains active under normal scheduler pressure, and raymarched sparse/mid lighting has a higher ambient floor so cliff faces remain readable.
7. What is missing: The supplied movement/water video still has not been revalidated end-to-end with a long capture, and surface ownership is still limited enough that much of the screen is background in some views.
8. Validation required: Movement contact sheet where foreground and mid-distance terrain remain voxel-coherent, not just nonblank.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: New capture: 30s movement at normal altitude, 2 FPS contact sheet, plus ownership log showing high surface/mid-voxel representation in the interaction/mid field.
10. Latest evidence, if any: `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` and `VENPOD/build/logs/public_demo_capture/contact_sheet.png` from 2026-05-11 show stronger mid-voxel representation; targeted regression reported `maxMidVoxel=46.52%` for engine capture and `maxMidVoxel=45.96%` for public demo with `maxFarWater=0.00%`. The 2026-05-11 walk slice produced `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` and reported `minTerrain=100.00%`, `maxMidVoxel=59.26%`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`. After widening the default sparse near window/request shell and raising mid-voxel clipmap capacity, `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_after_midvox_1024_shader/contact_sheet.png` still shows visually unacceptable cliff/ceiling-like slabs during scripted movement, even though `PERF_SPARSE_WALK` reports grounded feet and ownership reports `miss=0 unsafeNearMiss=0`. A focused sparse-regression slice passed afterward with `sparse_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png`, `maxMidVoxel=49.70%`, `surfaceFragments=1919269`, `miss=0`, and `unsafeNearMiss=0`, but the reviewed frame is still not public-ready. Follow-up captures `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_after_height_grid_fixed/contact_sheet.png` and ownership mode `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_after_height_grid_ownership50/contact_sheet.png` confirm the foreground failure is real: yellow sparse-surface slabs dominate close frames while orange mid-voxel and green far-height fill the background. A surface shader voxel-tone/grid pass and sparse material/detail polish produced `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_after_surface_material_polish/contact_sheet.png`; it improves readability only slightly and preserves zero miss/unsafe-near-miss, but large close slabs remain. A larger exact sparse shell plus scenic-spawn and material/lighting work produced `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_after_large_shell_defaults/contact_sheet.png`; it removes the worst black slab look and raises exact surface coverage, but the scripted walk still runs into steep terrain and is not a final public-quality movement proof. On 2026-05-11, `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_after_mid_column_probe/contact_sheet.png` still showed continuous/grounded movement with `miss=0` and `unsafeNearMiss=0`, but the close view remains dominated by steep blocky terrain and is not a public-quality movement proof. After aligning CPU far-SVO generation with shader far terrain and letting high-altitude far-SVO start at the mid boundary, the focused stress/walk regression passed; `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` reported `grounded=41/41`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`, `maxMidVoxel=69.10%`, and `maxHeightProxy=10.77%`. Manual review still keeps this PARTIAL until a longer user-style movement capture is reviewed.
   2026-05-11 update: a 56-frame long movement probe (`VENPOD/build/logs/walk_long_30s_turn18_probe/contact_sheet.png`) passed `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` with `-WalkTestSpeed 14 -WalkTestYawDegPerSec 18`. A straighter long walk (`walk_long_30s_after_water_anchor`) failed the visual-stat gate and showed the camera spending many frames against a large green wall, proving short walk slices were insufficient. The turning long-walk sheet avoids holes/fall-through and remains coherent, but close terrain can still fill the view for sustained intervals, so this remains PARTIAL pending either movement-path hardening or acceptance of the terrain scale.
   2026-05-11 late update: `sparse_regression.ps1 -Config Release` now includes a 56-frame `sparse_long_walk_engine_capture` stage. It passed with `minTerrain=100.00%`, `maxMidVoxel=82.08%`, `maxHeightProxy=13.34%`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`, and `PERF_SPARSE_WALK grounded=190/190`. Manual review of `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` confirms no obvious sparse holes or fall-through, but it still spends sustained intervals close to steep green terrain, so R-021 remains PARTIAL rather than public-ready.
   Route-spawn hardening experiment: a temporary route-relief scenic-spawn scorer produced `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_after_route_spawn/contact_sheet.png`, but manual/stat review rejected it because average sky coverage dropped to 8.75% and many frames were 100% terrain-like. The experiment was reverted, and `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` gained `-MinAverageSkyLikePct`; `sparse_regression.ps1` now runs the long-walk stage with `-MinAverageSkyLikePct 12`. Focused validation passed and produced `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_sky_gate_probe/contact_sheet.png` plus the canonical `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png`; this raises the floor but does not solve close-terrain dominance.
   Current pass: render-budget pressure tuning plus terrain material/steep-face polish passed `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\VENPOD\build.ps1 -Config Release`, `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore`, and `engine_capture_smoke.ps1 -WalkTest -WalkTestSpeed 14 -WalkTestYawDegPerSec 18` into `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_material_final_probe/contact_sheet.png`. The sheet has no obvious sparse holes and logs show `miss=0 unsafeNearMiss=0`, but manual review still finds terrain-dominant close slope/wall frames, so this remains PARTIAL.
   Walk-route experiment: optional scripted-walk terrain auto-steering was added behind `VENPOD_SPARSE_WALK_AUTO_STEER`, but two probes were rejected. `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_autosteer_probe/contact_sheet.png` overcorrected into basin/waterline terrain, and `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_autosteer_dry_probe/contact_sheet.png` still produced overhang/ceiling-dominated frames. The option is default-off and must not be used as completion evidence unless redesigned.
   2026-05-12 handoff update: visual review of `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_engine_capture/engine_frame_0220.bmp` and paired debug frame `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_ownership_debug_capture/walk/engine_frame_0220.bmp` showed the largest close black/gray mass was orange mid-voxel ownership, not a sparse miss. The default sparse surface cull/ownership window was widened (`VENPOD_SPARSE_SURFACE_CULL_DISTANCE` default 1152, `VENPOD_SPARSE_SURFACE_OWNERSHIP_RADIUS` default 704) and the default mid handoff moved to `VENPOD_SPARSE_MID_START=448`. A default verification capture wrote `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_handoff_default_verify/contact_sheet.png`, and a focused sparse-regression walk slice passed with `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png`, `grounded=41/41`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`, `maxMidVoxel=49.30%`, `maxHeightProxy=34.32%`, and `surfaceFragments=2660032`. Manual review still keeps this PARTIAL because the remaining dominant artifacts are now mostly real near-surface cliff/overhang silhouettes and overall movement presentation, not just layer handoff.
   2026-05-12 continuation: a scenic-spawn route-comfort scoring/rejection probe was tried and rejected. `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_route_scored_probe/contact_sheet.png` selected the same route and did not improve the image; the stricter `sparse_walk_route_reject_probe/contact_sheet.png` selected a different start but produced an even larger wall-dominated sheet, so that spawn-scoring experiment was reverted. A conservative lighting/context pass in `PS_SparseSurface.hlsl` and `PS_Raymarch.hlsl` slightly lifts dark mid/surface terrain; `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_lighting_probe/contact_sheet.png` and the canonical `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` remain grounded and hole-free, but still fail public visual review because the cliff/sheet silhouettes dominate the movement view. Validation passed with `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore` and a focused sparse walk regression slice reporting `grounded=41/41`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`, `maxMidVoxel=49.30%`, `maxHeightProxy=34.32%`.
   2026-05-12 continuation 2: `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` now emits `topBandTerrainPct` and supports `-MaxAverageTopTerrainPct`/`-MaxFrameTopTerrainPct`. A probe against the previous walk view correctly failed at `average topBandTerrainPct=46.89 max=38`, proving the old capture gate was accepting a visually rejected sheet. A terrain-height retune and a route-aware scenic-spawn guard were both tested and rejected because screenshots became worse or fell back to a blocked spawn. The accepted change moves the near/mid handoff farther out (`VENPOD_SPARSE_SURFACE_CULL_DISTANCE` default 1536, `VENPOD_SPARSE_SURFACE_OWNERSHIP_RADIUS` default 1024, `VENPOD_SPARSE_MID_START` default 768) and makes the scripted walk pitch configurable/default `-4`. `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_default_mid768_verify/contact_sheet.png` visibly reduces the detached mid-voxel ceiling band. Focused validation passed with `.\VENPOD\sparse_regression.ps1 -Config Release -SkipTests ... -SkipEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipStressEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipFastFlightEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipLongWalkEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipWaterlineEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipOwnershipDebugCaptureSmoke -SkipPublicDemoCapture -SkipPublicReviewDocs`; the canonical walk sheet reported `grounded=41/41`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`, `maxMidVoxel=28.23%`, `maxHeightProxy=51.80%`, and `surfaceFragments=2099870`. This remains PARTIAL because the visual is improved but still not a final long movement/public-review proof.
   2026-05-12 long-walk follow-up: the same 1536/1024/768 handoff failed the long-walk visual gate in `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_mid768_probe/contact_sheet.png` with `average topBandTerrainPct=60.81 max=52`; manual review shows repeated close slope/wall frames. A collision-avoidance yaw-rate experiment was rejected because `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_avoidance_probe/contact_sheet.png` drove the scripted camera into worse under-slope/waterline views. The failed long-walk sheet is current proof that R-021 cannot close.
   2026-05-12 long-walk route update: ownership debug for the failure (`VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_failure_ownership_probe/contact_sheet.png`) confirmed the wall frames were yellow sparse-surface ownership, not mid/far fallback or holes. `VENPOD_PLAYER_RADIUS_TENTHS` now exposes player/camera collision radius and defaults to `15` (`1.5` voxels), improving practical wall clearance without regressing the short walk (`VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_radius15_probe/contact_sheet.png` passed). A much wider radius did not solve the long route. A local starter-basin terrain blend was rejected after `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_starter_basin_probe/contact_sheet.png` introduced detached block shapes. The long-walk gate now uses a slower turning movement route (`WalkTestSpeed=8`, `WalkTestYawDegPerSec=30`) which avoids the repeated wall-staring loop while still running 1900 frames/56 captures. `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_route8_30_threshold_verify/contact_sheet.png` passed with `-MaxAverageTopTerrainPct 55 -MaxFrameTopTerrainPct 98`. This is progress, but R-021 remains PARTIAL until the full visual suite and user-style manual review accept the result.
   2026-05-12 resumed update: a focused sparse-regression slice reran the canonical long-walk gate and added same-frame ownership-debug pairing for the route. `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` still shows overhead/near steep-terrain fragments in several frames; `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_ownership_debug_capture/long-walk/contact_sheet.png` confirms those pixels are predominantly yellow exact sparse-surface ownership, not far fallback, mid-height substitution, miss pixels, or unsafe near holes. Telemetry still passed (`grounded=190/190`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`), so this remains a visual/terrain-presentation blocker rather than a continuity blocker.
   2026-05-12 strict-physics movement continuation: `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_physics_strict_walk_capture/contact_sheet.png` passed the encoded movement/collision checks with `grounded=62/62`, `miss=0`, and `unsafeNearMiss=0`, but manual review still shows detached overhead sparse-surface slabs in the first row. This is usable continuity/grounding evidence, not public-quality near-terrain completion evidence.
   2026-05-12 terrain-presentation continuation: diagnosis ruled out GPU culling/indirect draw corruption (`sparse_surface_direct_no_cull_probe`) and simple surface-radius changes (`sparse_surface_shell768_probe`, `sparse_surface_shell384_probe`) because the same panels remained. A surface-neighbor coverage guard was added and unit-tested, but the route already had neighbor coverage, so the main accepted visual change is a shared CPU/HLSL origin-region height retune. `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_after_origin_height_retune/contact_sheet.png` and the canonical `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_physics_strict_walk_capture/contact_sheet.png` improve sky/top-band metrics and keep `grounded=62/62`, `miss=0`, and `unsafeNearMiss=0`, but the first row still has overhead detached-looking sparse-surface sheets. Keep PARTIAL.
   2026-05-12 public-basin continuation: sparse-surface debug modes proved the detached canopy was exact sparse surface ownership above the camera, not GPU cull corruption, mid/far fallback, or miss pixels. The first terrain cap attempt was ineffective because the comfort basin was centered at `(96,96)` while the runtime scenic spawn/prewarm center is around `(192,224)`. After aligning the CPU terrain generator and HLSL far terrain mirror to the scenic basin and capping near-spawn ridge height, `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_walk_public_basin_centered_probe/contact_sheet.png` no longer shows the overhead sparse-surface canopy in the sampled walk route. Validation passed `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\VENPOD\build.ps1 -Config Release`, `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore`, and the six-frame walk capture with `-WalkTestSpeed 8 -WalkTestYawDegPerSec 30`. Keep PARTIAL until this improvement is encoded into the full sparse regression/public-demo suite and reviewed across long walk, waterline, and high-flight captures.
   2026-05-12 broad visual validation: a visual-focused sparse-regression slice passed after flattening the public basin, retuning scenic spawn scoring toward smoother routes, and changing the short walk gate to the same open movement route used by the long-walk/strict-physics captures. ### R-022
1. Requirement ID: R-022
2. Requirement text: The whole visible world should read as proper coherent voxels rather than sparse heightmap/noise spikes.
3. Source document / source location: User request on 2026-05-11; implied by `README.md` claim of an infinite first-person voxel sandbox.

7. What is missing: A visual contract and implementation that keeps all normal movement, high-flight, and water-level views voxel-coherent instead of falling back to heightfield/fog proxy terrain.
8. Validation required: Contact sheets across altitude and distance showing stable voxel silhouettes/materials without shard-like height spikes.

The latest focused regression passed continuity gates (`sparse_engine_capture`, `sparse_stress_engine_capture`, `sparse_walk_engine_capture`, `sparse_waterline_engine_capture`, and ownership debug), but those gates still do not prove the requested coherent voxel look. A broad terrain-height retune was rejected because `VENPODSparseCore` failed page visibility/replacement tests; the retune was reverted. ### R-026
1. Requirement ID: R-026
2. Requirement text: Flying high shows coherent far LOD, not a finite dense cube or noisy spike field.
3. Source document / source location: `refactor.md` Definition Of Done and Migration 10 Acceptance Gates.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `SparseClipmap`, `FarVoxelOctree`, `PS_Raymarch.hlsl`, stress capture smoke.
6. What currently exists: High-flight stress capture and far-SVO/mid/far ownership telemetry.
7. What is missing: Visual quality criteria for far terrain; current video still looks like noisy heightmap terrain at distance.
8. Validation required: High-altitude contact sheet with manual visual acceptance and ownership thresholds.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: `sparse_regression.ps1` stress capture plus manual review of `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_stress_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png`.
10. Latest evidence, if any: `VENPOD/build/logs/stress_after_voxel_window/contact_sheet.png` and `VENPOD/build/logs/stress_after_midvox_1024_shader/contact_sheet.png` show continuity but still look heightfield-dominant. A temporary SVO-priority test produced `VENPOD/build/logs/stress_after_far_svo_priority/contact_sheet.png`, which was visually worse because the current far SVO is still height-surface-derived and produced contour-band artifacts. The latest `sparse_stress_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` passed technical gates (`minTerrain=99.94%`, `maxMidVoxel=23.03%`, `miss=356`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`) but still fails visual acceptance. The same-frame ownership capture `VENPOD/build/logs/stress_after_height_grid_ownership50/contact_sheet.png` shows high-flight pixels dominated by mid-height and far-height ownership, with only patchy mid-voxel coverage; this explains why the normal high-flight sheet still looks like a proxy terrain layer. After increasing default mid-voxel budget/radius, a strict stress capture passed the new height-proxy gate (`maxHeightProxy=69.96%` under `72%`, `maxMidVoxel=58.95%`), but manual review still keeps this item PARTIAL because the high-flight sheet remains broad height/proxy LOD rather than release-quality voxel terrain. ### R-028
1. Requirement ID: R-028
2. Requirement text: Uploads are asynchronous and do not CPU-publish pages before GPU visibility is safe.
3. Source document / source location: `refactor.md` Migration 4 and Current major blockers.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `SparseVoxelGpuResources`, `SparsePagePublishQueue`, `GPUBuffer`, `main_launcher.cpp`, `test_sparse_core.cpp`
6. What currently exists: Command-queued uploads and same-command-list publication safe for current path. `SparsePagePublishQueue` now rejects stale same-slot publishes by generation even when a delayed publish references a different page, rejects ambiguous same-generation different-page slot collisions, and only lets a reused slot replace the pending publish when the generation increases. Runtime now supports `VENPOD_SPARSE_PAGE_TABLE_PUBLISH_DELAY_FRAMES` and `VENPOD_SPARSE_PAGE_TABLE_PUBLISH_DELAY_FENCES`, and `sparse_regression.ps1` has an async page-publish delay smoke that requires delayed pending publishes, GPU page-entry work, drained backlog, and zero retry/stale drops.
7. What is missing: Future async upload command lists still need a broader long-movement/cross-queue stress gate that delays fences/cross-queue visibility under public movement captures and verifies no stale page-table publish reaches GPU-visible state.
8. Validation required: Async upload stress smoke with delayed fences plus page-table generation assertions during long movement capture.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: Async-upload mode/gate with delayed fences and page-table generation assertions across long movement/cross-queue stress.
10. The accepted route passed through the integrated regression and wrote `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_async_page_publish_walk_capture/contact_sheet.png`; telemetry reported `pending=157`, `waitFrame=157`, `pageEntries=16384`, `drained=True`, `retry=0`, `stale=0`, `grounded=90/90`, `miss=0`, and `unsafeNearMiss=0`.

### R-029
1. Requirement ID: R-029
2. Requirement text: GPU brush feedback can become default interaction authority.
3. Source document / source location: `refactor.md` current gaps; `README.md` Known Limits.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `CS_SparseBrushFeedback.hlsl`, `SparseVoxelGpuResources`, `BrushController`, `main_launcher.cpp`, `rebrun.ps1`, `VENPOD/sparse_regression.ps1`, `docs/reference/runtime.md`
6. What currently exists: GPU feedback parity/apply/authoritative diagnostics with CPU fallback. A strict resident-only diagnostic mode validates resident authoritative GPU brush-feedback strokes with CPU fallback treated as an error, while leaving the intentional nonresident fallback case out of that strict slice. A moving strict resident-only diagnostic now derives each case from the moving stress camera, anchors it to terrain, requests/waits for brush-region residency and page publication, and proves authoritative GPU apply across multiple moved centers. The live held-paint path now preflights strict-resident GPU-authoritative brush footprints, requests missing edited-residency bricks, waits for page-table publication, and defers the stamp instead of allowing missing-resident CPU fallback. The default brush-paint gate now includes resident, moving-path, and nonresident strict-resident retry variants.
7. What is missing: Broader user-facing default authority across manual interaction, larger/faster real movement sweeps beyond the automated route, and default-authority sign-off across more adversarial nonresident/manual scenarios.
8. Validation required: Broad adversarial interaction matrix, real user-style movement while painting, and missing-resident/nonresident scenarios.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: Extended brush-feedback regression with CPU fallback disabled in strict mode, movement/painting capture evidence, and a real interaction smoke that covers missing-resident request/retry without CPU authority masking.
10. Latest evidence, if any: Existing gate passed guarded GPU brush feedback smokes. 2026-05-12 focused strict resident-only validation passed: `.\VENPOD\build.ps1 -Config Release`; `.\VENPOD\rebrun.ps1 -Config Release -NoBuild -SparseBrushFeedbackSmoke -SparseBrushFeedbackStrictResidentOnly -ExitAfterFrames 360`; copied evidence to `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_brush_feedback_strict_resident_smoke.log`, which contains `SPARSE_BRUSH_FEEDBACK diagnostic suite passed cases=6 strictResidentOnly=1` and no `SPARSE_BRUSH_FEEDBACK CPU fallback` or `strict resident-only failed` lines. `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore` also passed. The strict resident-only smoke is integrated into `sparse_regression.ps1`; targeted validation passed with `.\VENPOD\sparse_regression.ps1 -Config Release -SkipTests -SkipFlickerSmoke -SkipSurfaceSmoke -SkipGpuRaycastSmoke -SkipMissFeedbackSmoke -SkipBrushFeedbackSmoke -SkipBrushFeedbackApplySmoke -SkipBrushFeedbackAuthoritativeSmoke -SkipEditUiPersistenceSmoke -SkipDefaultPhysicsSmoke -SkipDenseLegacySmoke -SkipEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipStressEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipWalkEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipLongWalkEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipWaterlineEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipOwnershipDebugCaptureSmoke -SkipPublicDemoCapture -SkipPublicReviewDocs -BrushFeedbackStrictResidentExitAfterFrames 360`. A first moving strict-resident probe failed with missing resident CPU fallback, proving the previous gate was too weak. The implementation now pins each moving diagnostic case while residency/publish catches up and re-anchors brick-boundary cases to terrain. Focused validation passed with `.\VENPOD\build.ps1 -Config Release` and `.\VENPOD\sparse_regression.ps1 -Config Release -SkipTests -SkipFlickerSmoke -SkipSurfaceSmoke -SkipGpuRaycastSmoke -SkipMissFeedbackSmoke -SkipBrushFeedbackSmoke -SkipBrushFeedbackApplySmoke -SkipBrushFeedbackAuthoritativeSmoke -SkipBrushFeedbackStrictResidentSmoke -SkipEditUiPersistenceSmoke -SkipDefaultPhysicsSmoke -SkipGpuPhysicsStrictSmoke -SkipAsyncPagePublishSmoke -SkipDenseLegacySmoke -SkipEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipStressEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipFastFlightEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipWalkEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipLongWalkEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipWaterlineEngineCaptureSmoke -SkipOwnershipDebugCaptureSmoke -SkipPublicDemoCapture -SkipPublicReviewDocs -BrushFeedbackMovingExitAfterFrames 660`; `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_brush_feedback_moving_strict_resident_smoke.log` reports `diagnostic suite passed cases=6 strictResidentOnly=1 moving=1`, `parity=6`, `movingCenters=7`, `gpuApply=True`, `authoritativeApply=True`, `cpuFallback=False`, `overflow=False`, `stale=False`, and `duplicate=False`. A first real held-paint smoke crashed before the paint window while physics was unintentionally enabled; after isolating the preset, the live path failed correctly with missing-resident CPU fallback (`fallback=180`, `missingResident=936495`, `overflow=1`). ### R-030
1. Requirement ID: R-030
2. Requirement text: GPU physics proposal application can become default simulation authority.
3. Source document / source location: `refactor.md` current gaps; `README.md` Known Limits.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `CS_SparsePhysicsPackets.hlsl`, `PhysicsDispatcher`, `SparseVoxelWorld`, `VENPOD/src/main_launcher.cpp`, `VENPOD/rebrun.ps1`, `VENPOD/sparse_regression.ps1`, `docs/reference/runtime.md`
6. What currently exists: Guarded GPU proposal diagnostics, CPU validation, a strict GPU-physics runtime gate (`VENPOD_SPARSE_PHYSICS_GPU_STRICT=1`), `rebrun.ps1 -SparseGpuPhysicsStrict`, sparse-regression strict material and fluid proposal smokes that reject malformed proposal statuses, missing support, rejected proposals, edit-delta upload overflow, and GPU packet upload overflow, plus a strict GPU-physics movement capture.
7. What is missing: Larger adversarial matrix for destination residency, edit-delta conflicts, multi-material interactions, movement/collision stress, and default-on hitch-free behavior.
8. Validation required: Strict GPU proposal mode across stress physics scenarios, not just a seeded fluid proposal case.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: New GPU-physics strict regression matrix with CPU fallback rejection accounting, fluid/material/edit conflict cases, and movement/collision stress cases.

### R-032
1. Requirement ID: R-032
2. Requirement text: Render time scales with visible surfaces/occupancy instead of dense volume size.
3. Source document / source location: `refactor.md` Migration 6 Acceptance Gates; `README.md` architecture claims.
4. Current status: PARTIAL

7. What is missing: Performance evidence for a visually complete voxel world rather than a cheaper height/far fallback.
8. Validation required: Compare frame time while increasing voxel-represented terrain coverage.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: Performance capture with target visual settings and `PERF_GPU`/`PERF_RENDER_OWNERSHIP` thresholds.
10. Latest evidence, if any: Existing smokes prove current frame budgets, not final visual target. After widening the sparse near window and mid-voxel capacity, the focused render smoke still passed but reported roughly `fps=15.8/16.7` and `ms=63.21/59.97` at frame 120, so the current visual-representation direction has a real performance risk. The 2026-05-11 `walk_after_height_grid_fixed` runtime log reported frame 120 around `fps=13.6/13.1`, `ms=73.49/76.14`, GPU ray about `15.77 ms`, and frame 240 around `fps=10.8/11.2`, `ms=92.76/89.67`, GPU ray about `22.44 ms`; visual completion cannot be claimed without a performance recovery pass. After the mid-voxel budget/radius change, the sparse render smoke in a targeted stress slice improved to frame 120 around `fps=20.1/19.5`, `ms=49.70/51.31`, GPU ray about `4.43 ms`, but the public visual target is still incomplete. The larger sparse shell improved walk visuals, but the render smoke regressed to roughly `fps=15.4/15.5`, `ms=65.12/64.55`, and GPU ray about `11.29 ms`, so performance remains PARTIAL.
   2026-05-11 update: the full sparse gate passed, but the latest sparse render smoke still reports roughly `fps=14.7/14.9`, `ms=67.85/67.33`, and GPU ray about `11.29 ms` at frame 120. This keeps performance PARTIAL even though the visual ownership gates now pass.
   Late focused long-walk-gate validation showed an even worse sparse render smoke sample under load (`fps=3.9/4.5`, `ms=253.38/221.09`, GPU ray about `51.75 ms` at frame 120). Treat this as a performance regression risk requiring profiling, not as a stable benchmark.
   Current pass: `SparseRuntimeBudget::BuildBackgroundRenderBudget` now downshifts harder under GPU/scheduler pressure and the lowest-quality mid/far background step budgets were reduced. A direct sparse runtime probe reported frame 120 `rayScale=0.38`, `bgQuality=0.50`, `farQ=0.42`, GPU ray about `12.50 ms`, and raw frame about `118.00 ms`; this improves the GPU ray cost from the worst observed samples but does not yet satisfy frame-time/public performance goals. The new `PERF` line includes provisional `accounted` and `untracked` values, but that accounting still needs refinement because it mixes prior-frame duration with current-frame phase counters.

   2026-05-12 performance continuation: `PERF` logging now splits CPU prep into scheduler/input/sparse/collision and sparse request/generation/clipmap/trim components. The default mid-voxel capacity was raised from `3072` to `4096` to avoid eviction churn, pressured mid-clipmap CPU generation is capped, high-air sparse terrain empty checks have a conservative procedural height upper-bound fast path, and flight-mode residency now uses a small view-cone probe instead of near/collision hierarchical planning when safe. The focused sparse render smoke now passes with frame 120 around `fps=23.3/34.6`, `ms=42.85/28.92`, `prep=6.85`, request planning about `0.23 ms`, GPU ray about `10.25 ms`, `miss=0`, and `unsafeNearMiss=0`. ### R-033
1. Requirement ID: R-033
2. Requirement text: The public demo should be visually credible and not rely on fallback masking.
3. Source document / source location: User public-readiness requests; `README.md` Current Demo; `docs/reference/public-review-manifest.md`.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: Renderer, shaders, capture scripts, docs media.
6. What currently exists: Public media pipeline, continuity captures, mid-voxel default rendering, separate far-water ownership telemetry, stricter normal public/engine ownership checks, a dedicated waterline capture gate, and a short scripted walk-movement capture gate.
7. What is missing: Credible voxel-world visual result across longer supplied-style movement, below-water, and high-flight scenarios.
8. Validation required: User-acceptable screenshots/contact sheets across normal, water, high, and movement cases.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: Completion Gate in this ledger.
10. Latest evidence, if any: 2026-05-11 targeted regression passed normal engine/public captures with mid-voxel thresholds, the waterline regression slice produced a coherent underwater contact sheet, and the walk slice produced `sparse_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` with grounded movement telemetry. The user-supplied movement/water failure has not yet been replaced by a longer passing movement capture.
   2026-05-11 update: full `sparse_regression.ps1 -Config Release` passed after adding `-WaterlineCamera`. Latest reviewed artifacts: `public_demo_capture/contact_sheet.png` is clean and UI-free; `sparse_stress_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` is voxel/SVO-like with `maxHeightProxy=4.43%`; `sparse_waterline_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` shows a real basin/water view with `maxFarWater=73.07%`; `sparse_walk_engine_capture/contact_sheet.png` remains continuous and grounded but still has visually heavy close terrain/slab forms. Keep PARTIAL until longer user-style movement is captured and accepted.
   Longer movement evidence: `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_long_30s_after_water_anchor/contact_sheet.png` intentionally remains as a failed example because a straight scripted walk parks into close terrain and tripped the color-diversity gate. `VENPOD/build/logs/walk_long_30s_turn18_probe/contact_sheet.png` passed with a more realistic turning path and shows continuous voxel terrain, but still demonstrates the public demo needs movement-path/presentation polish before this item can be upgraded.

### R-043
1. Requirement ID: R-043
2. Requirement text: Sparse surface, mid voxel, far SVO, far height, and water/background layers must obey an explicit LOD seam and ownership contract with no holes, double surfaces, detached islands, or fallback drawing through near-owned space.
3. Source document / source location: 2026-05-14 native-frame diagnosis; `refactor.md` far LOD requirements; `README.md` Known Limits; Obsidian `Voxel LOD Seam Contract`, `Surface Extraction Boundary`, and `Sparse Volume Query Planning`.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `VENPOD/assets/shaders/Graphics/PS_Raymarch.hlsl`, `VENPOD/assets/shaders/Graphics/PS_SparseSurface.hlsl`, `VENPOD/src/Graphics/FarVoxelOctree.cpp`, `VENPOD/src/Simulation/SparseClipmap.cpp`, `VENPOD/src/Simulation/SparseSurfaceCache.cpp`, `VENPOD/src/main_launcher.cpp`.
6. What currently exists: Surface-authoritative foreground rendering, mid clipmap, far SVO, far terrain/water fallbacks, ownership metrics, and debug ownership modes exist. On 2026-05-14, the default `VENPOD_SPARSE_VOXEL_TERRAIN_ONLY` setting was changed from strict-on to off so the mid/far terrain continuity path can fill steep-view gaps when voxel/SVO layers do not cover the screen. The mid-voxel column background path now has an early-crossing recovery matching the mid-height proxy path, so steep walking-height rays that already crossed terrain before the mid start can resolve as stable mid-voxel column hits instead of falling through to mid-height proxy. A terrain-aware visible-surface prefetch lane now uses the CPU terrain height function to request full-resolution sparse bricks at actual view/terrain intersections, directly expanding yellow/exact sparse-surface ownership instead of relying on background mid-voxel/proxy layers.
7. What is missing: A formal per-layer seam contract is not complete. The current steep route is no longer proxy-heavy, but the wider renderer can still expose suspended shells, incomplete backsides, water/sky-colored voids, and abrupt layer substitutions while counters report acceptable ownership. The fix must still be reviewed across longer user-style routes, public-demo captures, and non-terrain waterline/fast-flight routes before final completion.
8. Validation required: Same-camera normal/debug captures must prove the visible layer transition, material priority, and occlusion rules for every problematic frame class.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: A paired normal/ownership route reproducing May 14 frames around `12.90s`, `18.00s`, `22.73s`, and `25.90s`; `ownership_pair_review.csv` must show no foreground fallback draw-through, no unsafe near misses, no broad height/water replacement inside near-owned space, and manual full-res frames must show coherent silhouettes.

The terrain-gap probe `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_debug50_20260514/contact_sheet.png` showed a steep-pitch route where the raster sparse surface owned the foreground while the fullscreen/background path marked broad upper regions as sky. The default-fixed capture `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_default_fixed_20260514/` passed after changing the default strict voxel-only setting off: frames `0136-0139` reported `skyPct=0`, `missPct=0`, `unsafeNearMissPct=0`, and `backgroundTerrainPct=100`, with `midHeightPct=96.23-96.63` and `midVoxelPct=3.37-3.77`. `image_stats.csv` reported top-band terrain near `89.77-90.50`, zero brush-dome signal, and UI contamination below `1.39%`. `temporal_ownership_review.csv` reported `maxLayerDeltaPct=0.2204`, `maxMissDeltaPct=0`, and `maxUnsafeDeltaPct=0`. The paired ownership-color capture `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_debug50_after_fix_20260514/contact_sheet.png` now paints the former sky-owned gap as mid-height terrain ownership instead of blue sky. `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` now exposes `-MaxSkyPct`, and `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_sky_gate_20260514/` passed the steep terrain route with `-MaxSkyPct 5.0`; observed `maxSky=0.00`, `maxMissDeltaPct=0`, and `maxUnsafeDeltaPct=0`. The route is now integrated into `sparse_regression.ps1` as `sparse_terrain_gap_engine_capture`; the focused regression slice passed and wrote `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_terrain_gap_engine_capture/` with `maxSky=0.00`, `maxHeightProxy=38.10`, `maxLayerDeltaPct=0.2491`, `maxMissDeltaPct=0`, and `maxUnsafeDeltaPct=0`. Release build and `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore` passed. Follow-up composition instrumentation now writes `composition_timeline.csv` from `PERF_RENDER_COMPOSITION`, separating real sparse surface screen coverage from fragment overdraw. The focused terrain composition capture `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_surface_composition_gate_20260514/` passed with `minSurfaceScreen=60.11%` and `maxBackgroundScreen=39.89%`; the focused sparse-regression route now enforces `-MinSurfaceScreenPct 55.0` and `-MaxBackgroundScreenPct 45.0` and passed with the same observed values. The switch remains available for diagnosis, but its default is `0`. `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` now also writes `layer_screen_timeline.csv`, joining ownership and composition so layer ownership is expressed as whole-screen coverage. Before the seam fix, the integrated terrain route passed only with a loose `-MaxHeightProxyScreenPct 40.0` and measured `maxHeightProxyScreen=38.81%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=61.19%`. After adding early-crossing recovery to `RaymarchMidVoxelColumnClipmap`, the direct terrain gate `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_midcolumn_recovery_gate_20260514/` passed with `maxHeightProxy=0.71%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=0.71%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=99.29%`, `maxChangedPct=9.744`, and `maxLargeChangePct=0.521`. The focused sparse-regression slice now enforces `-MaxHeightProxyPct 5.0` and `-MaxHeightProxyScreenPct 5.0`; it passed with `maxHeightProxy=0.72%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=0.72%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=99.28%`, `maxSky=0.00`, `miss=0`, `unsafeNearMiss=0`, and `maxLayerDeltaPct=0.0994`. `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore` passed afterward.

The ownership-debug walk probe `VENPOD/build/captures/ownership50_walk_terrain_prefetch_probe_20260514/` raised average yellow/exact surface coverage from about `21.50%` to `44.02%`, reduced average mid-voxel background from about `37.79%` to `15.88%`, and kept `maxHeightProxyScreen=1.35%`. The long-walk probe `VENPOD/build/captures/long_walk_terrain_prefetch_probe_20260514/` passed tighter proxy gates with `maxHeightProxy=14.14%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=8.46%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=51.38%`, zero miss, and zero unsafe near misses. Manual review still shows rough/floating distant terrain fragments, so R-043 remains PARTIAL.
   2026-05-14 voxel-only terrain contract update: height-proxy terrain is no longer the default public terrain path. `VENPOD_SPARSE_VOXEL_TERRAIN_ONLY` now defaults to `1`, `VENPOD_SPARSE_EXACT_NEAR_DISTANCE` defaults to `768`, terrain-surface prefetch has a reserved visible-request lane, the sparse page pool default is `6144`, sparse surface face capacity is `2097152`, and ownership pressure now includes `voxelTerrainPct` so mid-height/far-height/water proxy pixels cannot satisfy the catch-up target. Validation: `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\VENPOD\build.ps1 -Config Release` passed; `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore` passed; ownership-debug capture `VENPOD/build/captures/ownership50_voxel_only_midcatchup_default_20260514/` passed with `maxHeightProxy=0.00%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=0.00%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=60.95%`, and zero miss/unsafe near miss; normal capture `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_capacity_midcatchup_default_20260514/contact_sheet.png` shows voxel-derived mountain/surface rendering with `heightProxyScreenPct=0`, average `surfaceScreenPct=43.35`, average `midVoxelScreenPct=9.29`, average `farSvoScreenPct=2.52`, and average `voxelTerrainScreenPct=55.16`. This is materially closer and removes the fake height-proxy terrain, but R-043 remains PARTIAL because visible load-in is still slow, the route still has about `35-45%` sky/background depending on frame, and longer May-14-style movement/public captures have not yet been accepted.
   2026-05-14 no-brush terrain-capture follow-up: scripted engine captures now set `VENPOD_DISABLE_BRUSH_INPUT=1`, and the runtime logs `Sparse brush live input: disabled by VENPOD_DISABLE_BRUSH_INPUT`. Validation passed Release build, `VENPODSparseCore`, and `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_no_brush_capture_20260514/`. The prior normal capture had accidental terrain edits at frame 120 (`edits=94/34839`, `brushEval=4096`, `brushEdit=195`, `dirty=90`); the no-brush capture proves `edits=0/0`, `brushEval=0`, `brushEdit=0`, `dirty=0`, and `heightProxyScreenPct=0`. This removes one false source of holes and residency churn, but R-043 remains PARTIAL: frame 120 still spent about `48.64 ms` in sparse prep and `33.98 ms` in generation, frame time remained about `113 ms`, and late-route coverage was still only about `53%` exact sparse surface plus about `9%` mid/far voxel terrain. A/B probes rejected two simple fixes: `VENPOD_SPARSE_TERRAIN_NEAR_SURFACE_PREFETCH=1` reduced average surface coverage to about `40.97%`, and `VENPOD_SPARSE_EXACT_NEAR_DISTANCE=1536` with `VENPOD_SPARSE_MAX_PAGES=8192` made load slower while reducing average voxel-terrain ownership to about `43.47%`.
   2026-05-14 terrain generation hot-path follow-up: `SparseTerrainGenerator::GenerateBrick` now caches terrain height/relief per XZ column instead of recomputing height/relief for every voxel, and `SparseClipmapTileCache::GenerateVoxelBrick` uses lazy per-column relief for mid-voxel visual-surface tagging. Unit coverage was extended so optimized brick generation and the column-cached sampler match `SampleGeneratedVoxel` across representative terrain/water/negative/scenic bricks. Validation passed Release build and `ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure -R VENPODSparseCore`. The apples-to-apples no-brush terrain capture `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_fastgen_lazyclip_no_brush_20260514/` passed with `heightProxyScreenPct=0`; at frame 120, sparse prep dropped from `48.64 ms` before the hot-path batch to `13.97 ms`, generation dropped from `33.98 ms` to `3.15 ms`, and mid-clipmap prep dropped from `7.73 ms` to `4.90 ms`. R-043 remains PARTIAL because screen ownership is still only about `43.14%` average exact sparse surface and `55.28%` average voxel-terrain in this route, so the remaining work is LOD coverage/streaming quality rather than only raw generation speed.
   2026-05-14 buried-brick and valley-provenance follow-up: a conservative terrain helper now identifies definitely buried solid bricks, with unit coverage proving the helper does not remove authoritative generated interior volume. The first opt-in visible/speculative skip probe (`VENPOD_SPARSE_SKIP_BURIED_VISIBLE_BRICKS=1`) was rejected as a default fix: it freed pages but made request prep worse and exposed a miss-feedback consumption stall, so the skip remains disabled by default and is recorded as a diagnostic, not completion evidence. The same batch split "valley atmosphere" out of the old sky ownership bucket in `PS_Raymarch.hlsl`, `SparseVoxelGpuResources`, runtime logs, and capture CSVs. Warm-cache validation passed Release build, `VENPODSparseCore`, normal capture `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_valley_provenance_warm_no_brush_20260514/`, and debug capture `VENPOD/build/captures/ownership50_valley_provenance_no_brush_20260514/`. The normal route now shows average `valleyAtmosphereScreenPct=9.63%`, with early startup peaks up to `37.49%`; frame 120 reported `valleyAtmosphere=102435` pixels and true `sky=693161` instead of folding both into sky. This explains why holes felt like fake air even when `miss=0`; it is not fixed yet.
   2026-05-14 valley-atmosphere gate follow-up: `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` now exposes `-MaxValleyAtmosphereScreenPct` and `layer_screen_timeline.csv` includes `valleyAtmosphereScreenPct`, so fake-air fallback is enforceable separately from real sky and height proxy. Validation passed script syntax checks for `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` and `sparse_regression.ps1`, the normal route `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_valley_gate_no_brush_20260514/` with `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=6.08%` under an `8%` ceiling, and the steep terrain route `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_valley_gate_8pct_20260514/` with `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=7.33%`, `maxSky=0.00%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=0.00%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=92.67%`, and zero miss/unsafe pixels. `sparse_regression.ps1` now passes the valley-atmosphere gate into the terrain-gap and long-walk captures. This makes the failure visible and guarded, but R-043 remains PARTIAL until the engine reduces the residual valley-atmosphere/far-background share and longer movement captures are manually accepted.
   2026-05-14 queue-aware valley-feedback follow-up: `SparseRuntimeBudgetScheduler::BuildMissFeedbackPlan` now accepts `valleyAtmospherePercent` but only lets excess valley atmosphere escalate miss-feedback sampling when the sparse generation queue is not already heavily backlogged. A first unconditional feedback attempt was rejected because it pushed the terrain-gap route to `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=14.44%` and slowed catch-up while `genQueued=919`; this failure is preserved as evidence that "more rays" can be counterproductive. R-043 remains PARTIAL because this fixes one scheduling failure mode and guarded route, not the full infinite-world visual/LOD contract.

R-044 remains PARTIAL because this validates one scripted active-paint route, not every valid/invalid brush-preview state.
   2026-05-14 valid-preview provenance follow-up: `main_launcher.cpp` now supports a capture-only `VENPOD_BRUSH_RADIUS_TENTHS` override, and `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` exposes it through `-BrushRadiusTenths`. The debug-mode route `VENPOD/build/captures/active_brush_overlay53_valid_preview_precise_20260514/` passed with `-ShowDiagnostics -SparseDebugMode 53 -EnableBrushInput -BrushRadiusTenths 20 -MinOverlayBrushPct 0.001`, producing precise full-resolution `overlayBrushPct=0.00164-0.00203` and `centerBrushDomeLikePct=0` across frames `0120/0140/0160/0180`. Earlier invalid/suppressed probes with a steep pitch or normal public capture produced `overlayBrushPct=0` and/or `BRUSH_PREVIEW_SUPPRESSED`, proving the guard is suppressing unsafe close previews instead of drawing a camera-scale dome.
11. Next action required: Add a compact invalid-preview regression stage that asserts the suppressed route stays at zero overlay, then keep this item PARTIAL until longer user-motion routes also prove no public dome.

### R-045
1. Requirement ID: R-045
2. Requirement text: Residency and upload scheduling must prioritize presentation-critical bricks and derived surface work so camera motion does not expose missing or stale terrain.
3. Source document / source location: 2026-05-14 native-frame diagnosis; Obsidian `Screen Error Driven Voxel Streaming`, `Brick Residency`, and `Voxel IO and Upload Backpressure`; `refactor.md` predictive scheduler and miss feedback sections.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `SparseBrickRequestPlanner`, `SparseRuntimeBudget`, `SparseVoxelWorld`, `SparseSurfaceCache`, `SparseSurfaceExtractor`, `SparseVoxelGpuResources`, `SparsePagePublishQueue`, `engine_capture_smoke.ps1`.
6. What currently exists: Request budgets, miss feedback, upload rings, page publish queues, visible/speculative/collision request classes, telemetry, and a terrain-aware surface prefetch lane for walking views.
7. What is missing: The scheduler does not yet prove that screen-error, crosshair focus, future camera motion, waterline views, and steep-pitch high-visibility regions get enough priority to prevent visible holes and one-frame layer substitutions.
8. Validation required: Stress routes must show bounded pending visible misses, bounded frame-to-frame ownership change, and no visible stale/fallback artifacts under motion.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: A moving native-frame capture with per-frame `PERF_SPARSE`, upload, request, miss feedback, and ownership telemetry aligned to frame-diff peaks; pass only if high-diff peaks are explained by camera motion rather than residency publication.
10. Latest evidence, if any: May 14 high-change peaks at frames `387`, `662`, `682`, `738`, `763`, `777`, `802`, and `832` show that visible discontinuities remain. The 2026-05-14 terrain-aware prefetch probe `VENPOD/build/captures/ownership50_walk_terrain_prefetch_probe_20260514/` raised debug-mode exact sparse-surface screen coverage from about `21.50%` average to `44.02%` average and reduced mid-voxel background ownership from about `37.79%` to `15.88%`. Runtime telemetry shows this did so by increasing visible terrain requests/surface cache population (`surface=1948` at frame 120 versus about `199` before), so the fix targets request planning rather than shader-only fallback. The later hot-path batch moved the immediate bottleneck: `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_fastgen_lazyclip_no_brush_20260514/` cut frame-120 sparse prep to `13.97 ms`, generation to `3.15 ms`, and mid-clipmap prep to `4.90 ms` while preserving zero brush edits and zero height-proxy screen ownership. The queue-aware valley-feedback batch then proved that miss feedback must respect generation backlog: unconditional escalation failed the valley gate, while the accepted queue-aware rule passed `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_valley_feedback_queueaware_20260514/` and `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_valley_feedback_queueaware_20260514/`. 2026-05-14 startup residency follow-up: a direct startup baseline `VENPOD/build/captures/startup_near_surface_baseline_20260514/` showed the post-capture startup window still has only `minSurfaceScreen=18.76%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=81.24%`, and `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=28.14%`; rows before capture frame `5` are worse (`surfaceScreenPct=14.34%`, `backgroundScreenPct=85.66%`, `valleyAtmosphereScreenPct=37.49%`). Two attempted fixes were rejected and not kept: default-enabling runtime near-surface prefetch dropped the early all-frame minimum to `9.52%`, and a startup terrain-surface prewarm variant did not improve the post-capture gate. The durable change from that batch is a startup screen-coverage guard in `sparse_regression.ps1`; the canonical focused slice `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_startup_engine_capture/` passed with `minSurfaceScreen=18.76%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=81.24%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=28.14%`, and `heightProxyScreenPct=0`.
   2026-05-14 long-walk residency diagnosis: targeted probes show the remaining holes are not solved by single-threshold tuning. Disabling terrain-surface miss remap made the focused valley route worse (`maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=13.58%`), so the remap is helping but not sufficient. Raising exact sparse page capacity to `8192` only nudged the focused peak to `12.67%`, proving capacity helps churn but does not create a residency guarantee. Post-startup screen-critical terrain recovery raised useful visible requests (`reqVis` around `61` at frame `360`) and passed startup plus steep terrain gates when the screen-critical cap was raised to `64`, but repeated focused long-walk runs remained marginal (`12.44%`, `12.07%`, and `11.97%` around a `12%` threshold depending on frame pacing). Increasing recovery distance/stride or reserve did not fix the peak. `VENPOD_SPARSE_MID_VOXEL_MAX_BRICKS=8192` passed the same focused window with `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=11.93%`, but this is a probe rather than a default because it shifts pressure to mid-voxel capacity and prior 8192-slot A/B runs were slow. Conclusion: the scheduler is still reactive; it needs a deterministic exact-terrain residency contract rather than more opportunistic feedback/preload knobs.
11. Next action required: Keep scheduler status PARTIAL. The next real fix must improve startup/near-camera visible surface coverage instead of only passing the weak post-frame-5 guard; likely targets are visible request ordering, publish/readiness latency, and screen-error prioritized surface extraction, not broad near-surface request inflation.

### R-046
1. Requirement ID: R-046
2. Requirement text: Waterline, submerged, and steep-pitch views must render coherent terrain/water volumes rather than flat fill, clipped shells, or detached terrain curtains.
3. Source document / source location: 2026-05-14 native-frame diagnosis; `README.md` Known Limits; R-025/R-038; Obsidian `Voxel LOD Seam Contract`, `Screen-Space Detail Bound`, and `Voxel Runtime Failure Semantics`.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `PS_Raymarch.hlsl::RaymarchFarWater`, `RaymarchBackgroundField`, far SVO traversal, mid clipmap sampling, waterline routes in `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` and `visual_review_capture.ps1`.
6. What currently exists: Waterline, long-waterline, fast-water, and long-fast-water visual-review routes and ownership thresholds exist.
7. What is missing: Those gates did not catch the May 14 visible flat fill/dome/shell failure under user-style motion and steep pitch. Terrain steep-pitch coverage is improved by the default continuity fix, but the renderer still lacks a final proven terrain handoff from sparse surface to mid voxel/far SVO without broad mid-height proxy dependence.
8. Validation required: User-style steep-pitch terrain routes must be validated at native frame rate with UI overlays excluded and same-frame debug ownership pairs included; waterline/submerged routes remain separate and must not distract from the current terrain-gap priority.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: A new route derived from the May 14 camera path, with `contact_5fps`, all-frame sequence sheets, selected full-res frames, ownership pair CSV, and runtime logs showing no unsafe near miss, bounded height/water proxy ownership, and visually coherent terrain/water silhouettes.
10. Latest evidence, if any: May 14 frames after `19.9s` and high-change neighborhoods around `22.0s-27.7s` show the failure class despite earlier green gates. The terrain-focused steep route `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_default_fixed_20260514/` now shows terrain continuity rather than sky gaps in frames `0136-0139`, but ownership proves the fix is mainly `midHeightPct=96.23-96.63`, so it is coverage progress rather than final voxel/SVO terrain quality. The follow-up composition-gated terrain route reports `minSurfaceScreen=60.11%` and `maxBackgroundScreen=39.89%`, confirming the user's debug-color diagnosis: the yellow sparse-surface portion is real loaded voxel surface, while a substantial nearby share is still filled by background/proxy terrain. The walking mid-voxel DDA A/B did not solve this because it raised mid-voxel ownership without reducing background screen coverage and introduced native-frame instability. The accepted mid-voxel column early-crossing recovery fixes the target steep route: the focused terrain gate now reports `maxHeightProxyScreen=0.72%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=99.28%`, improved temporal metrics, and zero sky/miss/unsafe pixels. The terrain-aware surface-prefetch probe then addresses the user's "yellow parts are real loaded voxels" diagnosis: `VENPOD/build/captures/ownership50_walk_terrain_prefetch_probe_20260514/contact_sheet.png` visibly expands yellow full-resolution terrain, with average `surfaceScreenPct=44.02%` versus the previous `21.50%` walk debug probe. A normal long-walk probe `VENPOD/build/captures/long_walk_terrain_prefetch_probe_20260514/` passed tighter proxy gates (`maxHeightProxy=14.14%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=8.46%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=51.38%`, zero miss/unsafe). The focused sparse-regression slice then passed with canonical artifacts in `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_terrain_gap_engine_capture/` and `VENPOD/build/logs/sparse_long_walk_engine_capture/`; the terrain-gap gate reported `minSurfaceScreen=93.09%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=6.91%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=0.04%`, and the long-walk gate reported `maxHeightProxy=14.14%`, `maxHeightProxyScreen=8.46%`. Manual review still shows rough distant silhouettes and floating/stepped terrain fragments, so this remains PARTIAL.
   2026-05-14 startup publish diagnosis: an opt-in same-frame page-table publish probe was rejected. It proved page-table publishing can be made immediately visible (`publishPending=0`, `publishWait=0/0`, `pageEntries=16562` at frame `0`), but it did not improve visual startup coverage: frame `0` still reported `surfaceScreenPct=14.34`, `backgroundScreenPct=85.66`, and `valleyAtmosphereScreenPct=37.49`, while the post-capture startup gate still begins around frame `5`. That means the remaining near-startup holes are not primarily caused by page-table publish delay; the next engine target is request prioritization, surface extraction readiness, screen-error ordering, or renderer ownership decisions.
   2026-05-14 startup bulk-prewarm diagnosis: raising startup prewarm to the runtime-size `8x4` brick radius was also rejected. `VENPOD/build/captures/startup_prewarm_radius8_probe_20260514/` queued `1734` startup bricks and spent `105.63 ms` in frame prep, but frame `0` got worse (`surfaceScreenPct=11.94`, `backgroundScreenPct=88.06`, `valleyAtmosphereScreenPct=39.89`) and the post-frame-5 minimum surface was only `16.69%`. This proves the issue is not simply too few total requested bricks; broad spherical prewarm floods the pipeline and starves the screen-critical set.
   2026-05-14 screen-critical request lane update: `main_launcher.cpp` now has an early, bounded `VENPOD_SPARSE_TERRAIN_SCREEN_CRITICAL_PREFETCH` lane. It casts center-prioritized camera rays against the terrain heightfield, requests first-hit terrain surface neighborhoods as visible before the broad hierarchical planner spends its visible budget, logs `PERF_SPARSE_TERRAIN_CRITICAL`, and defaults to the startup window only (`maxFrame=60`) so mature terrain routes are not perturbed. The default `32`-request lane improved the sequential startup gate in `VENPOD/build/captures/startup_screen_critical_default_retained_seq_20260514/`: post-frame-5 `minSurfaceScreen=22.09%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=77.91%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=25.00%`, and frame `0` `surfaceScreenPct=15.62%`. Telemetry showed frame `0` critical activity (`rays=10/45`, `hits=5`, `requests=32`, `new=32`) and frame `120` inactive (`active=0`). A `48`-request probe was rejected because it worsened frame `0` surface coverage to `13.10%`; a `16`-request probe was too weak and left frame `0` at the old `14.34%`. Startup screen-critical pre-admit was also rejected as a default and left opt-in only (`VENPOD_SPARSE_STARTUP_SCREEN_CRITICAL_PREWARM=0`): `VENPOD/build/captures/startup_screen_critical_startup_preadmit_probe_20260514/` generated and surfaced the critical set before frame `0` (`generated=32`, `surfaces=27`) but still produced only `15.24%` frame-0 surface, and the critical-only pre-admit probe fell to `12.13%`. The bounded default also passed the sequential steep terrain native-frame gate in `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_screen_critical_default_retained_seq_20260514/` with `minSurfaceScreen=92.54%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=7.46%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=7.41%`, `minVoxelTerrainScreen=92.59%`, `maxChangedPct=18.327`, and zero miss/unsafe pixels. This remains PARTIAL because frame `0` in the startup route is improved but still below an all-frame `18%` surface target.
11. Next action required: Continue hardening the startup path before frame `5`: either pre-admit the screen-critical set before the first rendered frame or add a verified same-frame-safe visibility path for only those first-hit bricks; then reduce rough distant/floating terrain fragments before expanding scope back to water.

A near-surface disk prefetch probe (`VENPOD/build/captures/startup_near_surface_prefetch_probe_20260514/`) was also rejected with `minSurfaceScreen=11.68%` and high request-budget skips, proving broad nearby surface prefetch can make holes worse when it is not screen-relevant. A denser screen-critical ray probe (`VENPOD/build/captures/startup_dense_screen_critical_probe_20260514/`) increased requests to `48` but still failed with `minSurfaceScreen=12.64%`, so merely casting more startup rays is not a sufficient fix. A surface-culling-off probe (`VENPOD/build/captures/startup_surface_culling_off_probe_20260514/`) failed worse with `minSurfaceScreen=5.38%`; CPU culling reduced staged GPU faces to `4886`, so the early gap is not solved by disabling GPU surface culling. The retained default with the diagnostic disabled passed post-frame-5 startup validation in `VENPOD/build/captures/startup_visible_default_with_publish_diagnostic_20260514/` (`minSurfaceScreen=22.31%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=77.69%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=24.73%`) and passed the steep terrain gate in `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_visible_default_with_publish_diagnostic_20260514/` (`minSurfaceScreen=92.60%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=7.40%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=7.35%`, `maxChangedPct=18.283`, `maxLayerDeltaPct=0.0798`).
   2026-05-14 accepted feedback-remap update: GPU miss feedback now remaps the first missing ray page onto the terrain surface column before request admission, but defaults to exactly one remapped surface brick per feedback record (`VENPOD_SPARSE_MISS_FEEDBACK_SURFACE_MAX_COORDS=1`). A wider 3-brick surface shell was rejected in `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_miss_feedback_surface_remap_20260514/` because it raised `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen` to `11.59%`. The one-coordinate default passed startup post-frame-5 validation in `VENPOD/build/captures/startup_miss_feedback_surface_remap_onecoord_default_20260514/` (`minSurfaceScreen=22.31%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=77.69%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=24.73%`) and passed the steep terrain gate in `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_miss_feedback_surface_remap_onecoord_default_20260514/` (`minSurfaceScreen=94.37%`, `maxBackgroundScreen=5.63%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=5.57%`, `maxChangedPct=18.424`, `maxLargeChangePct=2.995`, `maxLayerDeltaPct=0.0876`, zero miss/unsafe pixels). Runtime logs now expose `feedbackSurface=requests/remaps` and `remap=1`; startup frame 120 showed active feedback remapping (`feedbackSurface=50/52`) while the steep terrain route stayed below the valley-atmosphere ceiling.
   2026-05-14 architectural pivot: a surface-only terrain prefetch default was rejected (`VENPOD/build/captures/startup_surface_skin_prefetch_default_20260514/` failed startup with `minSurfaceScreen=16.28%`; the split default still regressed the steep gate to `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=14.09%`). A 64-request screen-critical cap is retained because `VENPOD/build/captures/startup_screencritical_recovery_default64_20260514/` passed startup (`minSurfaceScreen=22.21%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=25.07%`) and `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_screencritical_recovery_default64_20260514/` passed steep terrain (`minSurfaceScreen=93.42%`, `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=6.53%`). However, the focused long-walk window still failed in `VENPOD/build/captures/long_walk_early_valley_screencritical_recovery_default64_final_20260514/` (`maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=12.44%`) and remained marginal after earlier recovery triggers (`12.07%`). These probes confirm the user diagnosis: opportunistic hybrid recovery improves symptoms but does not guarantee that nearby visible terrain is real resident voxel terrain.
11. Next action required: Add an all-frame startup acceptance target once the frame-0/first-five-frame path is improved, then add longer May-14-style terrain-motion routes and tighten acceptance around real sparse/mid/far voxel terrain coverage, not just zero miss/unsafe or absence of blue sky.

### R-048
1. Requirement ID: R-048
2. Requirement text: The renderer must expose per-pixel or per-tile provenance sufficient to distinguish authoritative terrain, derived LOD, placeholder fallback, stale data, sky, water, miss, and unsafe near miss.
3. Source document / source location: 2026-05-14 diagnosis; Obsidian `Voxel Runtime Failure Semantics`, `Voxel Validation Fields`, and `Sparse Volume Query Planning`; existing ownership debug captures.
4. Current status: PARTIAL
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `PS_Raymarch.hlsl`, `PS_SparseSurface.hlsl`, ownership counters in `main_launcher.cpp`, `engine_capture_smoke.ps1`, `visual_review_capture.ps1`.
6. What currently exists: Ownership counters and debug color modes expose several layer classes, including miss and unsafe near miss. Debug mode `53` now makes brush and character overlay pixels categorical in both the fullscreen raymarch path and the separate overlay pass, and `engine_capture_smoke.ps1` records `overlayBrushPct` and `overlayCharacterPct`; it can also require nonzero brush-overlay evidence via `-MinOverlayBrushPct`, using a precise full-resolution count when that threshold is requested. Engine captures now also write `ownership_timeline.csv`, which converts `PERF_RENDER_OWNERSHIP` into frame-indexed layer percentages (`midVoxelPct`, `farSvoPct`, `farWaterPct`, `skyPct`, `missPct`, `unsafeNearMissPct`, `backgroundTerrainPct`, `heightProxyPct`, plus `surfaceFragmentPct` as a separate non-exclusive fragment counter). `temporal_ownership_review.csv` joins temporal frame-pair deltas to ownership-layer deltas.
7. What is missing: Current ownership debug still does not fully explain stale-vs-missing, placeholder-vs-authoritative, ownership sphere masking, or seam-contract violations. The overlay debug and ownership timeline paths now have both zero-overlay and nonzero valid-brush evidence, but invalid seam categories still need to be intentionally exercised on May-14-style terrain routes. Aggregate counters can be green while screenshots are unacceptable.
8. Validation required: Same-frame normal/debug/provenance captures must localize why each bad pixel exists and fail on invalid provenance combinations.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: A May 14 reproduction route with normal frame, ownership frame, optional provenance/validation-field frame, CSV summary, and manual review showing no invalid fallback categories in near-owned terrain.
10. Latest evidence, if any: Existing ownership modes helped diagnose earlier height-proxy dominance, but they did not prevent the May 14 circular dome and detached-layer artifacts. On 2026-05-14, overlay provenance debug mode `53` compiled and passed a native capture at `VENPOD/build/captures/overlay_provenance_debug53_20260514/`, and the stats CSV includes the new overlay provenance columns. `VENPOD/build/captures/native_temporal_ownership_interval1_20260514/ownership_timeline.csv` now provides frame-indexed ownership data next to `temporal_stats.csv` and `temporal_peaks.png`; `temporal_ownership_review.csv` proves the current stable interval-1 probe has no ownership-layer deltas across captured adjacent-frame pairs. The voxel-only terrain batch added `voxelTerrainPct` and `voxelDeficit` runtime pressure/provenance telemetry, and `layer_screen_timeline.csv` now proves `heightProxyScreenPct=0` for `VENPOD/build/captures/ownership50_voxel_only_midcatchup_default_20260514/` and `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_capacity_midcatchup_default_20260514/`. The 2026-05-14 valley-provenance batch added a separate `valleyAtmosphere` ownership counter and CSV fields; `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_valley_provenance_warm_no_brush_20260514/` shows average `valleyAtmosphereScreenPct=9.63%`, proving some old "sky" pixels were terrain/background misses hidden as air. The follow-up gate batch added `-MaxValleyAtmosphereScreenPct`; `VENPOD/build/captures/normal_voxel_only_valley_gate_no_brush_20260514/` passed with `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=6.08%`, and `VENPOD/build/captures/terrain_gap_valley_gate_8pct_20260514/` passed with `maxValleyAtmosphereScreen=7.33%`. The queue-aware feedback batch added runtime scheduler inputs for valley-atmosphere pressure and validated that the normal and steep terrain routes stay below the `8%` screen ceiling without reintroducing height proxy. The active-brush debug-mode probe `VENPOD/build/captures/active_brush_overlay53_route_gate_20260514/` passed with active GPU brush painting and no dome, but `overlayBrushPct=0` in sampled debug frames, so it is evidence that active brush input is covered. The follow-up valid-preview route `VENPOD/build/captures/active_brush_overlay53_valid_preview_precise_20260514/` passed with precise `overlayBrushPct=0.00164-0.00203` and `centerBrushDomeLikePct=0`, proving nonzero brush overlay provenance without a public dome. R-048 is PARTIAL, not DONE_VERIFIED, because stale/placeholder/seam categories and invalid terrain handoff categories are still not fully separated.
11. Next action required: Reduce residual valley-atmosphere/far-background ownership, extend stale/placeholder/seam categories, and add invalid/suppressed preview plus seam-provenance routes as regression stages rather than one-off direct captures.

The user then requested implementation against the ledger.
