### R-050
1. Requirement ID: R-050
2. Requirement text: Default public terrain rendering needs a deterministic exact-terrain residency contract instead of a hybrid renderer that can hide nearby missing voxels with background, valley atmosphere, height proxy, far SVO, or water-like fallback.
3. Source document / source location: User follow-up on 2026-05-14 ("Nothing's working... bigger architectural issue"); R-043/R-045/R-047/R-048 evidence; Obsidian concepts `Voxel Runtime Failure Semantics`, `Screen Error Driven Voxel Streaming`, `Sparse Volume Query Planning`, and `Voxel LOD Seam Contract`.
4. Current status: NOT_STARTED
5. Related source files, modules, functions, scripts, or assets: `Simulation/SparseVoxelWorld.*`, `Simulation/SparseRuntimeBudget.*`, `Simulation/SparseBrickRequestPlanner.*`, `Simulation/SparseClipmap.*`, `Graphics/Renderer.*`, `assets/shaders/Graphics/PS_Raymarch.hlsl`, `assets/shaders/Graphics/PS_SparseSurface.hlsl`, `VENPOD/engine_capture_smoke.ps1`, and this ledger.
6. What currently exists: Exact sparse surfaces, mid-voxel/far-SVO context, terrain-surface prefetch, miss-feedback terrain remap, screen-critical startup/recovery prefetch, and validation gates for surface/background/valley ownership exist. These are useful components, but they are still opportunistic and reactive.
7. What is missing: A hard invariant that every pixel inside a chosen near/mid terrain radius is backed by loaded exact sparse terrain or by an explicitly valid voxel LOD handoff. There is no deterministic per-frame visible terrain working set, no admission barrier that prevents public fallback from standing in for nearby missing terrain, no stable terrain ring/clipmap contract with hysteresis, and no debug failure mode that marks illegal near-terrain fallback as a violation.
8. Validation required: A default-route capture suite must prove zero illegal fallback inside the exact-terrain radius across startup, steep pitch, long walk, high/fast flight, and waterline-adjacent terrain. The proof must include normal frames, provenance/debug frames, `layer_screen_timeline.csv`, runtime logs, and selected full-resolution failure/peak frames.
9. Exact command, test, artifact, screenshot, runtime output, log, or manual check that would prove completion: Implement a "guaranteed exact terrain radius" mode, then run a fresh Release build, `VENPODSparseCore`, a focused May-14 long-walk valley gate, the startup/steep terrain gates, and the full sparse regression. Completion requires all captures to show no near-radius valley/height/water/sky fallback, no miss/unsafe pixels, bounded temporal ownership deltas, and manual review that nearby world is real voxel terrain rather than a disguised background.
10. Latest evidence, if any: Current evidence proves the need, not completion. The best focused probes still oscillate around the failure threshold (`maxValleyAtmosphereScreen` near `12%`) depending on frame pacing, while capacity and recovery knobs only give partial gains. The 8192 mid-voxel probe can pass a focused route, but it does not establish exact terrain residency and is not promoted to default.
11. Next action required: Design and implement the first architecture slice: build a deterministic per-frame visible terrain surface set for a bounded radius, reserve generation/upload/surface extraction/page publish for that set before optional background layers, and add a provenance/debug gate that fails if nearby visible terrain falls through to valley/height/water/sky fallback.

## Highest-Priority Incomplete Items

1. R-050: Deterministic exact-terrain residency contract is NOT_STARTED and is now the main terrain fix path; incremental prefetch/recovery knobs are not enough.
2. R-043/R-047: Nearby steep terrain has moved from proxy-dominant continuity to guarded sparse/mid/far voxel coverage on focused routes, but repeated long-walk valley windows remain marginal and frame-pacing dependent.
3. R-043/R-048: Terrain layer seam/provenance must be made explicit before tuning thresholds; otherwise green counters can continue to hide background/valley fallback.
4. R-044: The first camera-scale dome guard is implemented and focused-capture verified, but remaining steep-pitch/user-motion brush valid/invalid routes are still needed because this artifact can hide every other visual problem.
5. R-022/R-026/R-038/R-043: Whole-world and far LOD still need coherent voxel/SVO terrain presentation rather than guardrail-compliant fallback.

## Items Most Likely To Cause Premature Closure

- R-005: Full sparse regression can pass while R-021/R-022/R-025 fail visually.
- R-020: `miss=0 unsafeNearMiss=0` proves continuity, not voxel coherence.
- R-007: Public media generation proves the pipeline works, not that the content is acceptable.
- R-026: Stress capture can prove infinite-looking terrain while still allowing unacceptable far LOD style.
- R-033: Public-readiness is broader than build/test/media generation.
- R-041: Native-frame analysis being complete does not mean the renderer is fixed.
- R-044: The focused dome guard capture is useful evidence, but it does not count as final completion until steep-pitch/user-motion brush valid/invalid states are covered.
- R-047: Contact sheets sampled every few frames can miss one-frame substitutions and overlay flashes, and ownership fragment counts can overstate real sparse surface coverage unless `composition_timeline.csv` is reviewed.
- R-050: Passing a focused valley window by increasing request caps or mid-voxel capacity is not completion unless the default renderer has a deterministic exact-terrain residency/provenance invariant.

## Validation Commands And Artifacts Needed For Final Completion

Baseline technical gates:

```powershell
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\VENPOD\build.ps1 -Config Release
ctest --test-dir .\VENPOD\build -C Release --output-on-failure
powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\VENPOD\sparse_regression.ps1 -Config Release
```

Required visual artifacts before final closure:

- Normal movement 30s capture, 2 FPS contact sheet, selected full-res frames, and the dedicated `sparse_walk_engine_capture` contact sheet.
- Water-level/submerged 30s capture, 2 FPS contact sheet, selected full-res frames, and the dedicated `sparse_waterline_engine_capture` and `sparse_long_waterline_engine_capture` contact sheets.
- High-flight/fast-flight capture, 2 FPS contact sheet, and selected full-res frames.
- Native-frame temporal analysis for startup, steep pitch, waterline/submerged, and fast camera motion routes, including peak-neighborhood contact sheets and per-frame metrics.
- Ownership-layer debug captures for the same camera windows: surface/near, mid voxel, mid height, far SVO, far height/water, sky, miss, unsafe near miss.
- Provenance/debug captures that distinguish authoritative, derived, placeholder, stale, overlay, sky/water, miss, and unsafe near-miss categories.
- Runtime logs for all captures with `PERF_RENDER_OWNERSHIP`, `PERF_SPARSE`, `PERF_SPARSE_SURFACE`, `midCov`, `farCov`, and water/LOD ownership if added.

## Exact Completion Gate

Do not mark the sparse renderer/public-readiness goal complete until all of the
following are true:

1. Every `DONE_UNVERIFIED`, `PARTIAL`, and `NOT_STARTED` item in this ledger that
   affects the default public sparse demo is either changed to `DONE_VERIFIED`
   with evidence or explicitly marked `DEFERRED_BY_USER_ONLY` by the user.
2. Build, CTest, and full sparse regression pass from a fresh Release build.
3. A normal movement capture, water/submerged capture, and high/fast-flight
   capture all have contact sheets, native-frame temporal metrics, selected
   full-resolution frames, and same-frame debug/provenance captures reviewed in
   this ledger or a successor ledger.
4. Those captures show no obvious gaps, no falling through terrain, no blue
   all-water/sky replacement when below water unless the camera is truly in open
   water, no detached far islands inside the editable shell, and no broad
   heightmap/noise-spike proxy where the expected view is coherent voxel terrain,
   and no brush-preview or ownership-volume dome in public frames.
5. Runtime ownership logs still show zero unsafe near misses, but final acceptance
   must additionally prove voxel/LOD representation quality, temporal stability,
   and valid layer provenance; zero miss pixels alone is not enough.
6. Startup/warmup frames meet the minimum-presentable residency contract instead
   of exposing striping or one-frame terrain publication pops.
7. Public docs and known limits match the actual visual state.
8. The worktree is organized into reviewable commits or a reviewable patch stack.
